```text
面向中学生的科普教学挂图，活泼的彩色涂鸦风格：纯净浅蓝底色，四周绘制黑色粗线条外框，模拟幻灯片版式。顶部中央用粗壮的手写感黑色字体书写标题「What is TNR?」。主体是四张横向排列的圆角矩形卡片：第一张画一只卡通猫走进笼门微开的诱捕笼，标注「1. Trap」；第二张画戴口罩手套的卡通兽医为猫做检查，标注「2. Neuter」；第三张画一只耳朵带有缺角标记的猫在阳光下伸懒腰，标注「3. Return」；第四张画几只健康的猫在社区草坪上晒太阳，标注「Healthy Cats」。全部文字为清晰的黑色无衬线体，笔触带厚重铅笔质感，色彩明快，信息通俗，适合课堂展示。
```

**English version**

```text
A colorful doodle-style teaching poster for middle-schoolers on a pale blue background framed by bold black lines like a slide. Hand-lettered title "What is TNR?" at top. Four rounded cards in a row: a cartoon cat entering a humane trap ("1. Trap"), a masked vet examining the cat ("2. Neuter"), an ear-tipped cat stretching in sunshine ("3. Return"), and healthy cats on a community lawn ("Healthy Cats"). Clean black sans-serif labels, thick pencil-like strokes, bright friendly palette.
```

src="images/education-cards/water-cycle-poster.webp" alt="The Water Cycle for Kids" width="640" /></a></p>

```text
为小学生设计的水彩手绘知识卡片：米白纸张质感打底，线条柔和、色彩通透。画面中心是一张由蓝色箭头首尾相连构成的圆形水循环流程图。顶部手写体主标题「自然界的水循环」，笔画圆润、边缘带淡淡水彩晕染。四个分区各配小标题、一句说明和一条趣味知识点：橙黄色水彩太阳下的「蒸发」——水分子化作微笑小水滴升上天空；高空棉絮状云朵的「凝结」；灰色乌云同落雨雪的「降水」；雨水汇成蜿蜒小河流回大海的「径流」。循环中央写「水的奇妙旅行」，底部一行总结语与趣味冷知识。整体以蓝色系为主、暖橙点缀，童趣盎然又知识完整。
```

**English version**

```text
A hand-painted watercolor knowledge card for primary-school students on cream paper. At center, a circular water-cycle diagram linked by blue arrows. Rounded handwritten title "The Water Cycle" with soft color bleeding. Four labeled zones — evaporation under a warm sun with smiling droplets rising, condensation into cotton-candy clouds, rain and snow falling together as precipitation, runoff weaving a stream back to the sea — each with a mini caption and one fun fact. Center label "Water's Amazing Journey", blue palette with warm orange accents.

src="images/education-cards/waste-sorting-hero.webp" alt="Waste Sorting Little Hero" width="640" /></a></p>

```text
儿童环保教育知识卡片：可爱手绘卡通风，浅色纸张质感底，线条圆润、纹理柔和。顶部是活泼跳色的大号手写标题「垃圾分类小能手！给地球洗个澡」。主体为 2×2 的四格彩色面板，每格里一只拟人化垃圾桶角色表情友善：蓝色面板「可回收物」，塑料瓶、旧报纸、压扁纸箱与汽水罐沿弧形箭头轻快飘入，配口诀「能卖钱的，洗净擦干」；红色面板「有害垃圾」，废电池、碎灯泡、过期药丸小心飘入，旁缀可爱小骷髅警示标，口诀「有毒有害，小心轻放」；绿色面板「厨余垃圾」与灰色面板「其他垃圾」同样各配角色、物品与口诀。字体圆润醒目、信息分区清晰，童趣十足又易记。
```

**English version**

```text
A hand-drawn cartoon recycling card for kids on light paper. Bouncy multicolor title "Waste Sorting Heroes — Give Earth a Bath!". A 2x2 grid of colored panels, each with a friendly anthropomorphic bin: blue recyclables with bottles and newspapers floating in on curved arrows, red hazardous waste with batteries and bulbs plus cute skull warnings, green kitchen waste and grey other waste, each with a catchy one-line rhyme. Rounded type, clear zones, playful and memorable.
```

卡通科普信息图，横向构图，明亮干净的扁平插画风格，适合健康教育课堂。
【标题】顶部居中粗体黑色大标题：「身体的防火演习：疫苗是如何工作的？」
【左区·演习阶段】黄色说明条：「疫苗送来『通缉令』和『假人靶子』，白细胞警察提前练兵，定制专属武器！」画面：卡通护士为手臂注射疫苗；旁边是「身体警察局」训练室——几位穿蓝色警服的圆滚滚白细胞警察，围着贴有「通缉令」的弹簧假人靶（标注「灭活病毒（假人靶子）」）训练 Y 形武器（标注「抗体（专属武器）」）；右下角黄色提示框：「身体记住了！武器准备就绪！」
【右区·实战阶段】白色说明条：「真病毒来袭！警察瞬间识别，用定制武器轻松获胜！」背景为粉橙渐变的人体通道剖面，几只穿紫红衣服、表情凶恶的拟人病毒（标注「真病毒（入侵者）」）被白细胞警察用 Y 形抗体围攻制服；右上角插一面黄色「WIN!」小旗。
【风格】色彩明快、线条干净、无医学恐怖感，拟人化比喻直观易懂，高分辨率清晰排版。

src="images/education-cards/newton-prism-chalkboard.webp" alt="Newton's Prism on a Chalkboard" width="640" /></a></p>

```text
黑板粉笔风格科学信息图，横向构图。
【背景】深色黑板，带粉笔灰、擦痕与手写颗粒质感，像中学科学教室的板书。
【标题】顶部居中彩色粉笔手写大标题：「牛顿的三棱镜实验：光的『七十二变』」，红蓝绿黄紫多色混用，边缘粗糙自然。
【主体】左侧白粉笔画矩形光源，射出一束水平白光，标注「一束白光（大礼包）」；中央白色粉笔线条勾勒透明三棱镜，带轻透视；右侧白光分解为红橙黄绿青蓝紫七道扇形彩束（粉笔质感渲染，非真实光效），右侧大括号标注「七色光谱（真相大白！）」，上下注「红光：拐弯最小」「紫光：拐弯最大」。
【趣味元素】三棱镜上方加两个红、紫拟人小粉笔人，像滑滑梯一样沿折射路径滑下，表情夸张。
【底部】粉笔讲解框，多行中文说明：「白光不是『单身』，而是七色合体的复色光——不同颜色性格不同（折射率不同），过三棱镜拐弯角度就不同！」
【风格】手绘抖动线条、粉笔颗粒感、信息结构清晰，教学感强、带幽默，非摄影非3D。
```

**English version**

```text
Chalkboard-style science infographic, landscape.
[BACKGROUND] Dark blackboard with chalk dust, eraser smudges and hand-drawn grain.
[TITLE] Multicolor hand-lettered chalk header: "Newton's Prism: White Light's Seven Disguises".
[BODY] Left: a chalk-drawn rectangular light source emitting a horizontal white beam labeled "a beam of white light"; center: a transparent prism outlined in white chalk with slight perspective; right: the beam fanning into red-orange-yellow-green-cyan-blue-violet chalk-textured rays, bracketed "the seven-color spectrum", with notes "red bends least / violet bends most".
[PLAYFUL BIT] Two tiny anthropomorphic chalk doodles (red and violet) slide down the refraction paths like a playground slide.
[BOTTOM] A chalk speech box explaining that white light is a combo of seven colors, each bending differently.
[STYLE] Quivering hand-drawn lines, chalk grain, clear teaching logic, a touch of humor; not photographic, not 3D.
```

src="images/education-cards/cpu-8bit-factory.webp" alt="CPU Explained: an 8-bit Game Factory" width="640" /></a></p>

```text
像素风格 8-bit 科普信息图，横向构图，模仿复古红白机说明书的视觉。
【标题】顶部粗体白色像素字、深色描边：「CPU 工作原理：冯·诺依曼架构（8-bit 游戏工厂）」，下方是深蓝到青绿的电路板底纹，布满像素电路线与发光节点。
【区域划分】左侧「控制单元（指挥官）」：像素机器人指挥官坐镇控制台，气泡「1. 取指（FETCH）」，台前像素卷轴标注「指令」；左下「解码器（翻译官）」：红色齿轮机器人展开卷轴，气泡「2. 解码（DECODE）」，卷轴分标「操作码」「地址」；中部横向像素传送带，标注「总线（传送带）」，载着「数据」「操作码」彩色方块流动；右上「寄存器（临时仓库）」：像素货架分 A、B 格存放彩色方块，旁注「快速存取」；右下「算术逻辑单元 ALU（计算器）」：红色机器人胸前带加减乘除面板、手持扳手，气泡「3. 执行（EXECUTE）」，传送带末端输出彩色方块，标注「结果」；中央偏上像素循环箭头，标注「循环（LOOP）」。
【风格】经典 8-bit 像素艺术，边缘清晰块状分明，信息密集但结构直观，中英对照标注，高分辨率。
```

**English version**

```text
8-bit pixel-art infographic, landscape, styled like a vintage console manual.
[TITLE] Chunky white pixel font with dark outline: "How a CPU Works: The Von Neumann Factory", over a deep-blue-to-teal circuit-board backdrop with pixel traces and glowing nodes.
[ZONES] Left: a pixel robot 'commander' at a console (Control Unit), speech bubble "1. FETCH"; below-left a red gear robot unrolls a scroll (Decoder), "2. DECODE", scroll split into 'opcode' and 'address'; across the middle a pixel conveyor belt labeled 'Bus' carrying data blocks; top-right a pixel shelf rack (Registers) with A/B slots, note 'fast access'; bottom-right a red robot with an arithmetic panel (ALU), "3. EXECUTE", outputting a result block; center-top a circular pixel arrow marked 'LOOP'.
[STYLE] Classic 8-bit pixels, crisp blocky edges, dense but readable, bilingual labels, high resolution.
```

```text
深海主题英文科普信息图，深蓝色调。
【标题】顶部居中白色粗体大标题「DEEP SEA ANGLER」。
【主体】画面中央一条大鮟鱇鱼：身体蓝紫渐变纹理，背部覆尖锐棘状背鳍，头部伸出发光的钓竿状诱球（esca），满口尖牙；周围均匀分布六个白边圆形信息框：上「HABITAT」含深海剖面小图（潜水器与小鱼）与文字「DEEP SEA 500m」；左上「REPRODUCTION」母鱼与幼鱼插画，文字「FEMALE LARGE-BODIED」；右上「DIET」鱼虾图标，文字「FISH & CRUSTACEANS」；左下「BIOLUMINESCENCE」灯泡图标，文字「ESCA (LURE ORGAN)」；右下「SIZE」鱼与直尺图标，文字「MAX LENGTH 1.2m」；底部「CONSERVATION」盾形对勾图标，文字「LEAST CONCERN」。
【背景】浮游生物、珊瑚与虚化小生物点缀深海环境，底部绘波浪状微光线模拟水流。
```

**English version**

```text
Deep-blue ocean infographic in English.
[TITLE] Bold white header centered: "DEEP SEA ANGLER".
[SUBJECT] A large anglerfish at center: blue-to-purple gradient skin, spiny dorsal fins, a long glowing lure (esca) extending from the head, a mouth full of fangs; six evenly spaced white-ringed circular frames around it: "HABITAT" with a deep-sea cross-section (submersible and small fish), text "DEEP SEA 500m"; "REPRODUCTION" with a female and juveniles, "FEMALE LARGE-BODIED"; "DIET" with fish and shrimp icons, "FISH & CRUSTACEANS"; "BIOLUMINESCENCE" with a bulb icon, "ESCA (LURE ORGAN)"; "SIZE" with fish-and-ruler icons, "MAX LENGTH 1.2m"; bottom "CONSERVATION" with a shield-check icon, "LEAST CONCERN".
[BACKGROUND] Plankton, corals and blurred creatures; wavy glimmering lines at the bottom suggest currents.
```

src="images/education-cards/muscle-imbalance-guide.webp" alt="What is Muscle Imbalance?" width="640" /></a></p>

```text
竖向卡通医学科普信息图，三段式分区布局，运动康复风格。
【标题区】顶部粗体黑色主标题「什么是『肌肉失衡』？」，下衬一行小字副标题「就像身体里的『拔河比赛』出了 bug！」。
【第一段·理想状态】浅绿渐变底：拟人化「关节君」（小机器人质感）站姿笔直表情开心，左右两块对称的橙色微笑肌肉向内均衡拉住它；气泡文案「左右力量相当」「关节稳定，活动自如！」。
【第二段·现实情况】偏黄橙的紧张底色：关节君表情惊慌身体被拉歪；左侧深红色异常壮硕的愤怒肌肉紧绷发力，气泡「这一侧：太强、太紧、太短！」；右侧瘦长蓝色肌肉被拉到变形、冒汗无力，气泡「另一侧：被迫拉长、无力！」；箭头标注「关节被拉歪，卡住了！」。
【第三段·后果】深灰蓝底：左侧简化人体剪影含胸驼背，肩颈腰髋以红色光点标出疼痛区；右侧白字说明「长期失衡导致体态变差、关节磨损、各种酸痛。」
【风格】卡通化、无写实解剖、无恐怖元素，色彩分区明确，文字层级清晰。
```

**English version**

```text
Vertical cartoon medical infographic in three stacked zones, rehab-education style.
[HEADER] Bold black title "What Is Muscle Imbalance?" with sub-line "like a tug-of-war inside your body gone wrong".
[ZONE 1 · IDEAL] Soft green gradient: a cheerful robot-like 'Joint-kun' standing straight, two symmetric smiling orange muscles pulling evenly from both sides; bubbles: "balanced strength" / "joint stable, moves freely".
[ZONE 2 · REALITY] Tense yellow-orange: Joint-kun alarmed and tilted; a bulky deep-red angry muscle yanks from the left ("too strong, too tight, too short!"), a stretched blue muscle strains weakly on the right ("forced long and powerless!"); arrow note "the joint gets dragged off-center and stuck".
[ZONE 3 · CONSEQUENCE] Deep gray-blue: a simplified hunched body silhouette with glowing red pain dots at neck, shoulder, lower back and hip; caption "long-term imbalance: worse posture, worn joints, chronic aches".
[STYLE] Fully cartoon, no realistic anatomy, no horror, clear color-coded zones and type hierarchy.
```

```text
科技感云平台架构示意图，浅灰网格底纹配发光粒子。
【中心】一枚大型立体云朵标注「Cloud Platform」，置于蓝紫渐变的圆形基座中央。
【左侧】「Compute Engine」由一簇编号立方体组成，向下依次为「Object Storage」（服务器阵列）与「SQL Database」（圆柱数据库）。
【右侧】「API Gateway」代码接口图标，其上下各一台带箭头的圆形「Network Router」。
【顶部】「Cloud」与「Multi-cloud」图标，彩色光束与下方相连。
【外围】台式机、笔记本、平板与手机围成一圈，各以彩色光束连入中央云；底部与右侧标注「User Access Layer」。整体为清爽的 3D 图标风格，科技蓝紫配色。
```

**English version**

```text
A tech-style cloud platform architecture diagram on a light-gray grid with glowing particles.
[CENTER] A large 3D cloud labeled "Cloud Platform" on a blue-purple gradient circular base.
[LEFT] "Compute Engine" as a cluster of numbered cubes, with "Object Storage" (server rack array) and "SQL Database" (cylinder DB) below.
[RIGHT] An "API Gateway" code-interface icon and two arrowed circular "Network Routers" above and below it.
[TOP] "Cloud" and "Multi-cloud" icons connected by colored beams.
[RIM] Desktops, laptops, tablets and phones arranged in a ring, each linked to the central cloud; "User Access Layer" labeled at bottom right. Clean 3D-icon style, techy blue-violet palette.
```

```text
面向中学生的科普教学挂图，活泼的彩色涂鸦风格：纯净浅蓝底色，四周绘制黑色粗线条外框，模拟幻灯片版式。顶部中央用粗壮的手写感黑色字体书写标题「What is TNR?」。主体是四张横向排列的圆角矩形卡片：第一张画一只卡通猫走进笼门微开的诱捕笼，标注「1. Trap」；第二张画戴口罩手套的卡通兽医为猫做检查，标注「2. Neuter」；第三张画一只耳朵带有缺角标记的猫在阳光下伸懒腰，标注「3. Return」；第四张画几只健康的猫在社区草坪上晒太阳，标注「Healthy Cats」。全部文字为清晰的黑色无衬线体，笔触带厚重铅笔质感，色彩明快，信息通俗，适合课堂展示。
```

**英文版 · English**

```text
A colorful doodle-style teaching poster for middle-schoolers on a pale blue background framed by bold black lines like a slide. Hand-lettered title "What is TNR?" at top. Four rounded cards in a row: a cartoon cat entering a humane trap ("1. Trap"), a masked vet examining the cat ("2. Neuter"), an ear-tipped cat stretching in sunshine ("3. Return"), and healthy cats on a community lawn ("Healthy Cats"). Clean black sans-serif labels, thick pencil-like strokes, bright friendly palette.

src="images/education-cards/water-cycle-poster.webp" alt="自然界的水循环" width="640" /></a></p>

```text
为小学生设计的水彩手绘知识卡片：米白纸张质感打底，线条柔和、色彩通透。画面中心是一张由蓝色箭头首尾相连构成的圆形水循环流程图。顶部手写体主标题「自然界的水循环」，笔画圆润、边缘带淡淡水彩晕染。四个分区各配小标题、一句说明和一条趣味知识点：橙黄色水彩太阳下的「蒸发」——水分子化作微笑小水滴升上天空；高空棉絮状云朵的「凝结」；灰色乌云同落雨雪的「降水」；雨水汇成蜿蜒小河流回大海的「径流」。循环中央写「水的奇妙旅行」，底部一行总结语与趣味冷知识。整体以蓝色系为主、暖橙点缀，童趣盎然又知识完整。
```

**英文版 · English**

```text
A hand-painted watercolor knowledge card for primary-school students on cream paper. At center, a circular water-cycle diagram linked by blue arrows. Rounded handwritten title "The Water Cycle" with soft color bleeding. Four labeled zones — evaporation under a warm sun with smiling droplets rising, condensation into cotton-candy clouds, rain and snow falling together as precipitation, runoff weaving a stream back to the sea — each with a mini caption and one fun fact. Center label "Water's Amazing Journey", blue palette with warm orange accents.
```

```text
儿童环保教育知识卡片：可爱手绘卡通风，浅色纸张质感底，线条圆润、纹理柔和。顶部是活泼跳色的大号手写标题「垃圾分类小能手！给地球洗个澡」。主体为 2×2 的四格彩色面板，每格里一只拟人化垃圾桶角色表情友善：蓝色面板「可回收物」，塑料瓶、旧报纸、压扁纸箱与汽水罐沿弧形箭头轻快飘入，配口诀「能卖钱的，洗净擦干」；红色面板「有害垃圾」，废电池、碎灯泡、过期药丸小心飘入，旁缀可爱小骷髅警示标，口诀「有毒有害，小心轻放」；绿色面板「厨余垃圾」与灰色面板「其他垃圾」同样各配角色、物品与口诀。字体圆润醒目、信息分区清晰，童趣十足又易记。
```

**英文版 · English**

```text
A hand-drawn cartoon recycling card for kids on light paper. Bouncy multicolor title "Waste Sorting Heroes — Give Earth a Bath!". A 2x2 grid of colored panels, each with a friendly anthropomorphic bin: blue recyclables with bottles and newspapers floating in on curved arrows, red hazardous waste with batteries and bulbs plus cute skull warnings, green kitchen waste and grey other waste, each with a catchy one-line rhyme. Rounded type, clear zones, playful and memorable.
```

【标题】顶部居中粗体黑色大标题：「身体的防火演习：疫苗是如何工作的？」
【左区·演习阶段】黄色说明条：「疫苗送来『通缉令』和『假人靶子』，白细胞警察提前练兵，定制专属武器！」画面：卡通护士为手臂注射疫苗；旁边是「身体警察局」训练室——几位穿蓝色警服的圆滚滚白细胞警察，围着贴有「通缉令」的弹簧假人靶（标注「灭活病毒（假人靶子）」）训练 Y 形武器（标注「抗体（专属武器）」）；右下角黄色提示框：「身体记住了！武器准备就绪！」
【右区·实战阶段】白色说明条：「真病毒来袭！警察瞬间识别，用定制武器轻松获胜！」背景为粉橙渐变的人体通道剖面，几只穿紫红衣服、表情凶恶的拟人病毒（标注「真病毒（入侵者）」）被白细胞警察用 Y 形抗体围攻制服；右上角插一面黄色「WIN!」小旗。
【风格】色彩明快、线条干净、无医学恐怖感，拟人化比喻直观易懂，高分辨率清晰排版。
```

src="images/education-cards/newton-prism-chalkboard.webp" alt="黑板粉笔风：牛顿三棱镜实验" width="640" /></a></p>

```text
黑板粉笔风格科学信息图，横向构图。
【背景】深色黑板，带粉笔灰、擦痕与手写颗粒质感，像中学科学教室的板书。
【标题】顶部居中彩色粉笔手写大标题：「牛顿的三棱镜实验：光的『七十二变』」，红蓝绿黄紫多色混用，边缘粗糙自然。
【主体】左侧白粉笔画矩形光源，射出一束水平白光，标注「一束白光（大礼包）」；中央白色粉笔线条勾勒透明三棱镜，带轻透视；右侧白光分解为红橙黄绿青蓝紫七道扇形彩束（粉笔质感渲染，非真实光效），右侧大括号标注「七色光谱（真相大白！）」，上下注「红光：拐弯最小」「紫光：拐弯最大」。
【趣味元素】三棱镜上方加两个红、紫拟人小粉笔人，像滑滑梯一样沿折射路径滑下，表情夸张。
【底部】粉笔讲解框，多行中文说明：「白光不是『单身』，而是七色合体的复色光——不同颜色性格不同（折射率不同），过三棱镜拐弯角度就不同！」
【风格】手绘抖动线条、粉笔颗粒感、信息结构清晰，教学感强、带幽默，非摄影非3D。
```

**英文版 · English**

```text
Chalkboard-style science infographic, landscape.
[BACKGROUND] Dark blackboard with chalk dust, eraser smudges and hand-drawn grain.
[TITLE] Multicolor hand-lettered chalk header: "Newton's Prism: White Light's Seven Disguises".
[BODY] Left: a chalk-drawn rectangular light source emitting a horizontal white beam labeled "a beam of white light"; center: a transparent prism outlined in white chalk with slight perspective; right: the beam fanning into red-orange-yellow-green-cyan-blue-violet chalk-textured rays, bracketed "the seven-color spectrum", with notes "red bends least / violet bends most".
[PLAYFUL BIT] Two tiny anthropomorphic chalk doodles (red and violet) slide down the refraction paths like a playground slide.
[BOTTOM] A chalk speech box explaining that white light is a combo of seven colors, each bending differently.

```text
像素风格 8-bit 科普信息图，横向构图，模仿复古红白机说明书的视觉。
【标题】顶部粗体白色像素字、深色描边：「CPU 工作原理：冯·诺依曼架构（8-bit 游戏工厂）」，下方是深蓝到青绿的电路板底纹，布满像素电路线与发光节点。
【区域划分】左侧「控制单元（指挥官）」：像素机器人指挥官坐镇控制台，气泡「1. 取指（FETCH）」，台前像素卷轴标注「指令」；左下「解码器（翻译官）」：红色齿轮机器人展开卷轴，气泡「2. 解码（DECODE）」，卷轴分标「操作码」「地址」；中部横向像素传送带，标注「总线（传送带）」，载着「数据」「操作码」彩色方块流动；右上「寄存器（临时仓库）」：像素货架分 A、B 格存放彩色方块，旁注「快速存取」；右下「算术逻辑单元 ALU（计算器）」：红色机器人胸前带加减乘除面板、手持扳手，气泡「3. 执行（EXECUTE）」，传送带末端输出彩色方块，标注「结果」；中央偏上像素循环箭头，标注「循环（LOOP）」。
【风格】经典 8-bit 像素艺术，边缘清晰块状分明，信息密集但结构直观，中英对照标注，高分辨率。
```

**英文版 · English**

```text
8-bit pixel-art infographic, landscape, styled like a vintage console manual.
[TITLE] Chunky white pixel font with dark outline: "How a CPU Works: The Von Neumann Factory", over a deep-blue-to-teal circuit-board backdrop with pixel traces and glowing nodes.
[ZONES] Left: a pixel robot 'commander' at a console (Control Unit), speech bubble "1. FETCH"; below-left a red gear robot unrolls a scroll (Decoder), "2. DECODE", scroll split into 'opcode' and 'address'; across the middle a pixel conveyor belt labeled 'Bus' carrying data blocks; top-right a pixel shelf rack (Registers) with A/B slots, note 'fast access'; bottom-right a red robot with an arithmetic panel (ALU), "3. EXECUTE", outputting a result block; center-top a circular pixel arrow marked 'LOOP'.
[STYLE] Classic 8-bit pixels, crisp blocky edges, dense but readable, bilingual labels, high resolution.

```text
深海主题英文科普信息图，深蓝色调。
【标题】顶部居中白色粗体大标题「DEEP SEA ANGLER」。
【主体】画面中央一条大鮟鱇鱼：身体蓝紫渐变纹理，背部覆尖锐棘状背鳍，头部伸出发光的钓竿状诱球（esca），满口尖牙；周围均匀分布六个白边圆形信息框：上「HABITAT」含深海剖面小图（潜水器与小鱼）与文字「DEEP SEA 500m」；左上「REPRODUCTION」母鱼与幼鱼插画，文字「FEMALE LARGE-BODIED」；右上「DIET」鱼虾图标，文字「FISH & CRUSTACEANS」；左下「BIOLUMINESCENCE」灯泡图标，文字「ESCA (LURE ORGAN)」；右下「SIZE」鱼与直尺图标，文字「MAX LENGTH 1.2m」；底部「CONSERVATION」盾形对勾图标，文字「LEAST CONCERN」。
【背景】浮游生物、珊瑚与虚化小生物点缀深海环境，底部绘波浪状微光线模拟水流。
```

**英文版 · English**

```text
Deep-blue ocean infographic in English.
[TITLE] Bold white header centered: "DEEP SEA ANGLER".
[SUBJECT] A large anglerfish at center: blue-to-purple gradient skin, spiny dorsal fins, a long glowing lure (esca) extending from the head, a mouth full of fangs; six evenly spaced white-ringed circular frames around it: "HABITAT" with a deep-sea cross-section (submersible and small fish), text "DEEP SEA 500m"; "REPRODUCTION" with a female and juveniles, "FEMALE LARGE-BODIED"; "DIET" with fish and shrimp icons, "FISH & CRUSTACEANS"; "BIOLUMINESCENCE" with a bulb icon, "ESCA (LURE ORGAN)"; "SIZE" with fish-and-ruler icons, "MAX LENGTH 1.2m"; bottom "CONSERVATION" with a shield-check icon, "LEAST CONCERN".
[BACKGROUND] Plankton, corals and blurred creatures; wavy glimmering lines at the bottom suggest currents.
```

src="images/education-cards/muscle-imbalance-guide.webp" alt="什么是「肌肉失衡」？" width="640" /></a></p>

```text
竖向卡通医学科普信息图，三段式分区布局，运动康复风格。
【标题区】顶部粗体黑色主标题「什么是『肌肉失衡』？」，下衬一行小字副标题「就像身体里的『拔河比赛』出了 bug！」。
【第一段·理想状态】浅绿渐变底：拟人化「关节君」（小机器人质感）站姿笔直表情开心，左右两块对称的橙色微笑肌肉向内均衡拉住它；气泡文案「左右力量相当」「关节稳定，活动自如！」。
【第二段·现实情况】偏黄橙的紧张底色：关节君表情惊慌身体被拉歪；左侧深红色异常壮硕的愤怒肌肉紧绷发力，气泡「这一侧：太强、太紧、太短！」；右侧瘦长蓝色肌肉被拉到变形、冒汗无力，气泡「另一侧：被迫拉长、无力！」；箭头标注「关节被拉歪，卡住了！」。
【第三段·后果】深灰蓝底：左侧简化人体剪影含胸驼背，肩颈腰髋以红色光点标出疼痛区；右侧白字说明「长期失衡导致体态变差、关节磨损、各种酸痛。」
【风格】卡通化、无写实解剖、无恐怖元素，色彩分区明确，文字层级清晰。
```

**英文版 · English**

```text
Vertical cartoon medical infographic in three stacked zones, rehab-education style.
[HEADER] Bold black title "What Is Muscle Imbalance?" with sub-line "like a tug-of-war inside your body gone wrong".
[ZONE 1 · IDEAL] Soft green gradient: a cheerful robot-like 'Joint-kun' standing straight, two symmetric smiling orange muscles pulling evenly from both sides; bubbles: "balanced strength" / "joint stable, moves freely".
[ZONE 2 · REALITY] Tense yellow-orange: Joint-kun alarmed and tilted; a bulky deep-red angry muscle yanks from the left ("too strong, too tight, too short!"), a stretched blue muscle strains weakly on the right ("forced long and powerless!"); arrow note "the joint gets dragged off-center and stuck".
[ZONE 3 · CONSEQUENCE] Deep gray-blue: a simplified hunched body silhouette with glowing red pain dots at neck, shoulder, lower back and hip; caption "long-term imbalance: worse posture, worn joints, chronic aches".
[STYLE] Fully cartoon, no realistic anatomy, no horror, clear color-coded zones and type hierarchy.
```

src="images/education-cards/cloud-platform-diagram.webp" alt="云平台架构图" width="640" /></a></p>

```text
科技感云平台架构示意图，浅灰网格底纹配发光粒子。
【中心】一枚大型立体云朵标注「Cloud Platform」，置于蓝紫渐变的圆形基座中央。
【左侧】「Compute Engine」由一簇编号立方体组成，向下依次为「Object Storage」（服务器阵列）与「SQL Database」（圆柱数据库）。
【右侧】「API Gateway」代码接口图标，其上下各一台带箭头的圆形「Network Router」。
【顶部】「Cloud」与「Multi-cloud」图标，彩色光束与下方相连。
【外围】台式机、笔记本、平板与手机围成一圈，各以彩色光束连入中央云；底部与右侧标注「User Access Layer」。整体为清爽的 3D 图标风格，科技蓝紫配色。
```

**英文版 · English**

```text
A tech-style cloud platform architecture diagram on a light-gray grid with glowing particles.
[CENTER] A large 3D cloud labeled "Cloud Platform" on a blue-purple gradient circular base.
[LEFT] "Compute Engine" as a cluster of numbered cubes, with "Object Storage" (server rack array) and "SQL Database" (cylinder DB) below.
[RIGHT] An "API Gateway" code-interface icon and two arrowed circular "Network Routers" above and below it.
[TOP] "Cloud" and "Multi-cloud" icons connected by colored beams.
[RIM] Desktops, laptops, tablets and phones arranged in a ring, each linked to the central cloud; "User Access Layer" labeled at bottom right. Clean 3D-icon style, techy blue-violet palette.
```
